Motivation: 传统原对偶内点法在处理KKT条件时显式处理互补条件，导致在解附近出现病态问题，限制了方法的数值稳定性和求解精度，尤其在大规模问题中难以实现高精度求解

Method: 引入辅助变量并通过回缩映射将其与对偶变量和松弛变量关联，使用softplus函数替代常用的指数映射来隐式满足互补条件，构造谱有界的KKT系统

Result: 新方法消除了原对偶内点法最紧迫的限制——解附近的病态问题，KKT系统保证谱有界，使得线性系统求解更稳定，支持无分解迭代方法、间接求解器，并能在低精度算术中实现高精度

Conclusion: 这种新视角为内点法开辟了新机遇，特别适用于大规模问题的高精度求解，通过隐式处理互补条件和保证KKT系统谱有界，解决了传统方法的数值稳定性问题

Abstract: This paper introduces a new method for solving quadratic programs using primal-dual interior-point methods. Instead of handling complementarity as an explicit equation in the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions, we ensure that complementarity is implicitly satisfied by construction. This is achieved by introducing an auxiliary variable and relating it to the duals and slacks via a retraction map. Specifically, we prove that the softplus function has favorable numerical properties compared to the commonly used exponential map. The resulting KKT system is guaranteed to be spectrally bounded, thereby eliminating the most pressing limitation of primal-dual methods: ill-conditioning near the solution. These attributes facilitate the solution of the underlying linear system, either by removing the need to compute factorizations at every iteration, enabling factorization-free approaches like indirect solvers, or allowing the solver to achieve high accuracy in low-precision arithmetic. Consequently, this novel perspective opens new opportunities for interior-point methods, especially for solving large-scale problems to high precision.

</details>


### [154] [Revisiting the Constant-Rank Constraint Qualification for Second-Order Cone Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00365)
*Nguyen Huy Chieu,Nguyen Thi Quynh Trang,Nguyen Thi Hai Yen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了二阶锥规划中的常数秩约束规范(CRCQ)，在线性情况下分析了其性质，证明了CRCQ与度量次正则约束规范(MSCQ)的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然Andreani等人引入的二阶锥规划CRCQ具有一些良好性质（如保证强二阶必要条件、独立于Robinson约束规范），但与经典非线性规划版本不同，它在线性情况下可能失效，且不清楚CRCQ是否蕴含MSCQ。本文旨在在线性二阶锥规划中深入分析CRCQ的性质。

Method: 首先证明面部常数秩性质在线性情况下不一定成立；然后推导出可行点满足该性质的充要条件；接着建立CRCQ的易验证特征；最后利用该特征证明CRCQ与MSCQ的等价性。

Result: 1) 面部常数秩性质在线性二阶锥规划中不一定成立；2) 获得了可行点满足该性质的充要条件；3) 建立了CRCQ的易验证特征；4) 证明了在线性二阶锥规划中，CRCQ与MSCQ是等价的。

Conclusion: 本文在线性二阶锥规划背景下全面分析了CRCQ的性质，澄清了其与经典非线性规划版本的重要差异，并最终证明了CRCQ与MSCQ的等价性，为二阶锥规划的理论分析提供了重要工具。

Abstract: The constant rank constraint qualification (CRCQ) for second-order cone programs, introduced by Andreani et al. in [Math. Program. 202 (2023), 473 - 513], shares some desirable properties with its classical nonlinear programming counterpart; specifically, it guarantees strong second-order necessary conditions for optimality, and is independent of the Robinson constraint qualification. However, unlike the classical version, this new CRCQ can fail in the linear case, and it is unclear whether CRCQ implies the metric subregularity constraint qualification (MSCQ). The aim of this paper is to examine the CRCQ for second-order cone programs in the linear setting. First, we show that the facial constant rank property, which is a key requirement for the validity of CRCQ, does not always hold in this context. Then, we derive a necessary and sufficient condition for a feasible point to satisfy this property. After that, we establish an easily verifiable characterization of CRCQ. Finally, utilizing this characterization, we prove that CRCQ and MSCQ are equivalent.

</details>


### [155] [A Musielak-Orlicz approach for modeling uncertainties in long-memory processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00407)
*Hidekazu Yoshioka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Musielak-Orlicz空间的状态依赖散度函数方法，用于同时评估supOU过程中回复测度和Lévy测度的不确定性，并将其转化为求解一对优化问题以获得累积量的上下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对长记忆过程（特别是supOU过程）中不确定性建模的挑战，传统方法如Kullback-Leibler散度无法正确定义相关优化问题，需要开发新的数学框架来同时处理回复测度和Lévy测度的不确定性。

Method: 使用Musielak-Orlicz空间上的状态依赖散度函数来同时评估supOU过程中回复测度和Lévy测度的不确定性，将问题转化为求解一对优化问题以获得累积量的上下界，并给出了求解这些优化问题的充分条件。

Result: 证明了经典散度函数（如Kullback-Leibler散度）无法正确定义优化问题，而通过适当的Musielak-Orlicz空间可以解决这一缺陷；提供了求解优化问题的充分条件；在水环境问题中展示了该框架的计算应用。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于Musielak-Orlicz空间的新数学方法，为研究长记忆过程及其应用提供了新视角，能够有效处理supOU过程中回复测度和Lévy测度的联合不确定性。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel mathematical approach for modeling uncertainties in long-memory processes. We focus on the superposition of Ornstein-Uhlenbeck processes, the so-called supOU process, as a nominal model for complex phenomena having long memory. Uncertainties in a supOU process appear as distortions of its reversion and Levy measures, and we propose to simultaneously assess uncertainties of these measures by using state-dependent divergence functions on Musielak-Orlicz spaces. Our approach is reduced to solving a pair of optimization problems that give upper- and lower-bounds of the cumulants of supOU processes subject to a prescribed uncertainty set. We show that classical divergence functions, such as the Kullback-Leibler one, fail to well-define these optimization problems and how this drawback can be resolved by using proper Musielak-Orlicz spaces. Moreover, we provide sufficient conditions to solve these optimization problems. We demonstrate a computational application of the proposed framework to water environmental problems where the streamflow discharge is modeled as a supOU process. This paper provides a new viewpoint for studying long-memory processes along with applications.

</details>


### [156] [Polynomial Stability for Weakly Coupled System with Partial Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00521)
*Bopeng Rao,Qiong Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 弱耦合系统在少量局部或边界控制下的稳定性分析：标量方程指数稳定可推出耦合系统多项式稳定，衰减率与方程数量无关


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究一般弱耦合系统在减少局部或边界控制下的稳定性问题，旨在理解当系统只有有限控制时，如何通过耦合效应传递阻尼以实现整体稳定

Method: 基于Kalman秩条件，利用预解估计和Kalman秩条件的巧妙运用，确保阻尼在耦合方程间的有效传递，并通过谱分析验证衰减率的最优性

Result: 在Kalman秩条件下，基础标量方程的指数稳定性可保证完整耦合系统的多项式稳定性，且衰减率不随系统方程数量变化；结果应用于多种具体模型

Conclusion: 弱耦合系统在有限控制下可通过Kalman秩条件实现有效阻尼传递，获得与标量方程相同的多项式衰减率，这一理论框架适用于广泛的物理系统

Abstract: We study the stability of general weakly coupled systems subject to a reduced number of local or boundary controls. We show that, under Kalman's rank condition, the exponential stability of the underlying scalar equation implies polynomial stability of the full coupled system. Moreover, the decay rate remains unchanged regardless of the number of equations in the system. The proof relies on resolvent estimates and a clever exploitation of Kalman's rank condition to ensure effective transmission of damping across the coupled equations. The abstract result is applied to several concrete models, including systems of wave equations with local viscous, local viscoelastic, or boundary damping; systems of plate equations with internal damping; and thermoelastic systems of type III. Moreover, the optimality of the decay rate is established via spectral analysis.

</details>


### [157] [An Accelerated Proximal Bundle Method with Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00569)
*Zhuoqing Zheng,Junshan Yin,Shaofu Yang,Xuyang Wu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Proximal bundle methods (PBM) are a powerful class of algorithms for convex optimization. Compared to gradient descent, PBM constructs more accurate surrogate models that incorporate gradients and function values from multiple past iterations, which leads to faster and more robust convergence. However, for smooth convex problems, PBM only achieves an O(1/k) convergence rate, which is inferior to the optimal O(1/k^2) rate. To bridge this gap, we propose an accelerated proximal bundle method (APBM) that integrates Nesterov's momentum into PBM. We prove that under standard assumptions, APBM achieves the optimal O(1/k^2) convergence rate. Numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed APBM.

</details>


### [158] [Theoretical Perspectives on Jabr-Type Convex Relaxations for AC Optimal Power Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00664)
*Gabor Riccardi,Ambrogio Maria Bernardelli,Stefano Gualandi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文系统综述了Jabr二阶锥松弛的强化技术，基于多线性等式建立了统一框架，将循环约束重新解释为多线性一致性条件，并分析了原始McCormick松弛与对偶扩展公式之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中Jabr松弛的强化技术（如循环约束、多线性项凸包、对偶重构）往往独立提出，缺乏统一的几何和图论基础。需要建立系统框架来揭示这些技术之间的内在联系，为设计更强松弛提供理论基础。

Method: 基于多线性等式建立统一视角：将循环约束重新解释为多线性一致性条件；运用经典凸包理论分析其凸化；研究原始McCormick松弛与对偶扩展公式的关系；识别这些松弛重合的结构条件；澄清交互图凸化与可行集凸化的区别。

Result: 建立了连接图结构、多线性凸化和锥松弛的统一框架，提供了现有结果的概念综合，并为设计更强松弛提供了新见解。特别阐明了在何种结构条件下不同强化技术会重合，以及交互图凸化与可行集凸化的本质区别。

Conclusion: 该论文通过多线性等式视角系统整合了Jabr松弛的各种强化技术，揭示了它们共同的几何和图论基础，为交流最优潮流问题的凸松弛研究提供了统一的理论框架和新的研究方向。

Abstract: The alternating current optimal power flow problem is a fundamental yet highly nonconvex optimization problem whose structure reflects both nonlinear power flow physics and the topology of the underlying network. Among convex relaxations, the second-order cone relaxation introduced by Jabr has proven particularly influential, serving as a computationally efficient alternative to semidefinite relaxations and a foundation for numerous strengthening techniques. In recent years, a variety of approaches have been proposed to tighten Jabr-type relaxations, including cycle-based constraints, convex envelopes of multilinear terms, and dual reformulations. However, these developments are often presented independently, concealing their common geometric and graph-theoretic foundations. This paper provides a structured review of strengthening techniques for the Jabr relaxation and develops a unifying perspective based on multilinear equalities. We reinterpret cycle constraints as multilinear consistency conditions, analyze their convexification through classical convex hull theory, and investigate the relationship between primal McCormick relaxations and dual extended formulations. In particular, we identify structural conditions under which these relaxations coincide and clarify the distinction between convexifying the interaction graph and convexifying the feasible set of the ACOPF. The resulting framework connects graph structure, multilinear convexification, and conic relaxations in a unified manner, offering both a conceptual synthesis of existing results and new insights for the design of stronger relaxations.

</details>


### [159] [Distributed partial state estimation for linear state-space systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00680)
*Juhi Jaiswal,Thomas Berger,Nutan Kumar Tomar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了线性时不变分布式状态空间系统的部分状态估计问题，建立了基于系统系数矩阵简单秩判据的充要条件，并提出了估计器参数矩阵的设计算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性时不变分布式状态空间系统的部分状态估计问题，旨在为这类系统建立理论分析框架和实用设计方法。

Method: 建立了基于系统系数矩阵简单秩判据的充要条件，要求通信图要么是有向、平衡且强连通的，要么是无向且连通的。通过简单矩阵理论获得估计器参数矩阵。

Result: 建立了部分状态估计的充要条件，提出了估计器参数矩阵的设计算法，并通过数值示例验证了理论结果和设计算法的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究为线性时不变分布式状态空间系统的部分状态估计问题提供了完整的理论分析和实用设计方法，所提出的秩判据和算法具有理论价值和实际应用意义。

Abstract: This study is concerned with the problem of partial state estimation for linear time-invariant (LTI) distributed state-space systems. A necessary and sufficient condition is established in terms of a simple rank criterion involving the system coefficient matrices, provided the communication graph is either directed, balanced and strongly connected or undirected and connected. The estimator parameter matrices are obtained by simple matrix theory. Finally, a numerical example demonstrates the feasibility and effectiveness of the proposed theoretical results and design algorithm.

</details>


### [160] [Chvátal-Gomory Rounding of Eigenvector Inequalities for QCQPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00932)
*Santanu S. Dey,Nan Jiang,Aleksandr Kazachkov,Andrea Lodi,Gonzalo Muñoz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出并分析了一类用于非凸二次约束优化问题(QCQPs)的有效不等式——特征-CG不等式，通过Chvátal-Gomory舍入和Burer-Letchford结果构建，证明了其与Boros-Hammer不等式的关系，并发现稀疏不等式比密集不等式更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非凸二次约束优化问题(QCQPs)，现有松弛方法存在局限性，需要开发更有效的有效不等式来提升求解效率。特别是实践中观察到稀疏不等式比密集不等式更有效，需要理论解释和系统方法。

Method: 1. 提出特征-CG不等式：通过对QCQPs的特征向量不等式应用Chvátal-Gomory舍入，并利用Burer-Letchford结果将二元有效不等式转换到连续设置；2. 定义三个嵌套子族并证明包含关系；3. 证明其中两个子族的凸锥闭包相等且与Boros-Hammer不等式一致；4. 分析密集特征-CG不等式在标准SDP+McCormick松弛中的无效性；5. 开发计算策略寻找稀疏特征-CG割并验证其有效性。

Result: 1. 建立了特征-CG不等式与Boros-Hammer不等式的关系；2. 证明了密集特征-CG不等式在标准松弛中无效，解释了实践中稀疏不等式更有效的原因；3. 开发的计算策略验证了稀疏特征-CG割的有效性，特别是稀疏不等式能显著改善对偶界，而密度会快速降低有效性。

Conclusion: 特征-CG不等式为QCQPs提供了新的有效不等式框架，理论分析揭示了稀疏性对不等式有效性的关键作用，计算策略证实了稀疏特征-CG割能显著提升非凸QCQP实例的求解性能，为实际应用提供了理论指导和实用工具。

Abstract: We introduce and analyze a class of valid inequalities for nonconvex quadratically constrained optimization problems (QCQPs) which we call Eigen-CG inequalities. These inequalities are obtained by applying a Chvátal-Gomory (CG) rounding to the well-known eigenvector inequalities for QCQPs, and transferring binary-valid inequalities to the continuous setting via a result of Burer and Letchford (2009). We define three nested subfamilies and prove that they are strictly contained in one another. However, we show that the convex conic closure of two of these subfamilies is equal and, in fact, coincides with the Boros-Hammer inequalities -- a powerful family of inequalities that include, in particular, the triangle and McCormick inequalities. Using this CG perspective, we also prove that dense Eigen-CG inequalities are ineffective when used with the standard SDP+McCormick relaxation. This provides a complementary perspective on what is observed in practice: that sparse inequalities are impactful. Finally, based on these insights, we develop a computational strategy to find sparse Eigen-CG cuts and verify their effectiveness in nonconvex QCQP instances. Our results confirm that density quickly degrades effectiveness, but that including sparse inequalities beyond triangle inequalities can provide significant improvements in dual bounds.

</details>


### [161] [Stability, Contraction, and Controllers for Affine Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01070)
*L. P. Wieringa,A. Padoan,F. Dorfler,J. Eising*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文针对仿射行为系统，建立了独立于特定表示的必要充分条件，解决了收缩性、参考轨迹可实现性以及线性/仿射反馈控制实现等三个基本稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动控制的兴起重新激发了行为系统理论的兴趣，但现有成果主要局限于线性系统，限制了方法在复杂行为中的适用性。仿射系统作为自然中间类别，既保留了线性化特征，又捕捉了本质非线性效应，同时具备足够的分析设计结构。

Method: 采用行为系统理论框架，针对仿射行为系统推导独立于特定表示的必要充分条件。研究内容包括：输入输出系统收缩性的逆Lyapunov定理、规定收缩参考轨迹的可实现性与存在性、以及这些参考轨迹能否通过线性或仿射反馈控制实现。

Result: 建立了仿射行为系统三个基本稳定性问题的必要充分条件：1) 输入输出系统收缩性的逆Lyapunov定理；2) 规定收缩参考轨迹的可实现性与存在性；3) 线性控制器足以实现收缩闭环，而仿射控制器则用于平衡点配置。

Conclusion: 仿射行为系统为复杂系统分析提供了有效框架，线性控制器能实现收缩闭环，仿射控制器则处理平衡点配置问题，为数据驱动控制方法向更广泛非线性系统扩展提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Recent developments in data-driven control have revived interest in the behavioral approach to systems theory, where systems are defined as sets of trajectories rather than being described by a specific model or representation. However, most available results remain confined to linear systems, limiting the applicability of recent methods to complex behaviors. Affine systems form a natural intermediate class: they arise from linearization, capture essential nonlinear effects, and retain sufficient structure for analysis and design. This paper derives necessary and sufficient conditions independent of any particular representation for three fundamental stability problems for affine behaviors: (i) converse Lyapunov theorems for contraction of input-output systems; (ii) implementability and existence of prescribed contractive references; and (iii) whether these references can be implemented with linear or affine feedback control. For the latter, we show that linear controllers suffice for implementing contractive closed-loop, and and affine controllers are needed for equilibrium placement.

</details>


### [162] [Spectral Decomposition of Discrete-Time Controllability Gramian and Its Inverse via System Eigenvalues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01149)
*Alexey Iskakov*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了离散时间线性动力系统Gramian矩阵的闭式谱分解框架，提供可控性Gramian及其逆的显式分解，得到这些矩阵的模式分解表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Gramian分析通常使用聚合特征（如特征值、奇异值、行列式、迹等），缺乏对Gramian内部结构的描述。本文旨在提供一种能够描述Gramian内部结构的方法，通过单个模式及其成对组合的贡献来揭示Gramian的内在特性。

Method: 开发了离散时间线性动力系统Gramian矩阵的闭式谱分解框架。主要结果提供了离散时间可控性Gramian及其逆的显式分解，用动力学矩阵的特征值表示，得到这些矩阵的模式分解表示。框架进一步扩展到离散时间Lyapunov差分方程的求解，并将所得公式置于更广泛的时变和非线性系统分析计算背景中。此外，分解推广到动力学矩阵具有多重特征值的系统，实现了特征模之间共振效应闭式估计。

Result: 获得了可控性Gramian及其逆的显式谱分解，提供了Gramian内部结构的模式分解表示。框架能够描述单个模式及其成对组合对Gramian的贡献，支持对共振相互作用的闭式估计。结果适用于多重特征值系统，为离散时间系统的可控性、可观性和稳定性分析提供了结构工具。

Conclusion: 提出的谱分解框架为离散时间系统的可控性、可观性和稳定性分析提供了结构工具，补充了现有基于Gramian的模型降阶、估计、执行器和传感器选择以及能量感知控制方法。除了理论意义外，推导的分解可能支持改进计算程序和开发更具信息性的性能标准，适用于广泛的离散时间控制问题。

Abstract: This paper develops a closed-form spectral decomposition framework for the Gramian matrices of discrete-time linear dynamical systems. The main results provide explicit decompositions of the discrete-time controllability Gramian and its inverse in terms of the eigenvalues of the dynamics matrix, yielding a mode-resolved representation of these matrices. In contrast to the more common use of aggregate Gramian characteristics, such as eigenvalues, singular values, determinants, and trace-based metrics, the proposed approach describes the internal structure of the Gramian itself through contributions associated with individual modes and their pairwise combinations. The framework is extended further to the solution of the discrete-time Lyapunov difference equation, placing the obtained formulas in a broader context relevant to the analysis and computation of time-varying and nonlinear systems. In addition, the decomposition is generalized to systems whose dynamics matrix has multiple eigenvalues, enabling a closed-form estimation of the effects of resonant interactions between eigenmodes. The proposed results provide a structural tool for the analysis of controllability, observability and stability in discrete-time systems and complement existing Gramian-based methods used in model reduction, estimation, actuator and sensor selection, and energy-aware control. Beyond their theoretical interest, the derived decompositions may support the development of improved computational procedures and more informative performance criteria for a range of discrete-time control problems.

</details>


### [163] [Affine Normal Directions via Log-Determinant Geometry: Scalable Computation under Sparse Polynomial Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01163)
*Yi-Shuai Niu,Artan Sheshmani,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种矩阵无关的LogDet方法，将仿射法向量的计算从需要三阶导数简化为二阶结构，实现了高效的可扩展实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿射法向量提供了内在的仿射不变下降方向，但长期以来其实际应用受到需要计算三阶导数和求逆切Hessian矩阵的限制，在高维情况下计算成本过高。

Method: 证明了仿射法向量计算可以精确简化为二阶结构：经典的三阶收缩项正是切Hessian矩阵对数行列式的梯度。基于此简化，开发了精确和随机的矩阵无关评估程序，针对稀疏多项式目标，利用导数的代数闭包特性实现了高效的稀疏核。

Result: MF-LogDet方法能以接近机器精度重现基于自动微分的原始仿射法向量方向，在中高维度下带来显著的运行时改进，在维度和稀疏性方面都表现出经验性的近线性扩展。

Conclusion: 该研究为仿射法向量评估提供了实用的计算途径，揭示了仿射微分几何、对数行列式曲率和大规模结构化优化之间的新联系。

Abstract: Affine normal directions provide intrinsic affine-invariant descent directions derived from the geometry of level sets. Their practical use, however, has long been hindered by the need to evaluate third-order derivatives and invert tangent Hessians, which becomes computationally prohibitive in high dimensions. In this paper, we show that affine normal computation admits an exact reduction to second-order structure: the classical third-order contraction term is precisely the gradient of the log-determinant of the tangent Hessian. This identity replaces explicit third-order tensor contraction by a matrix-free formulation based on tangent linear solves, Hessian-vector products, and log-determinant gradient evaluation. Building on this reduction, we develop exact and stochastic matrix-free procedures for affine normal evaluation. For sparse polynomial objectives, the algebraic closure of derivatives further yields efficient sparse kernels for gradients, Hessian-vector products, and directional third-order contractions, leading to scalable implementations whose cost is governed by the sparsity structure of the polynomial representation. We establish end-to-end complexity bounds showing near-linear scaling with respect to the relevant sparsity scale under fixed stochastic and Krylov budgets. Numerical experiments confirm that the proposed MF-LogDet formulation reproduces the original autodifferentiation-based affine normal direction to near machine precision, delivers substantial runtime improvements in moderate and high dimensions, and exhibits empirical near-linear scaling in both dimension and sparsity. These results provide a practical computational route for affine normal evaluation and reveal a new connection between affine differential geometry, log-determinant curvature, and large-scale structured optimization.

</details>
